{"thought": "I'll implement the OPCA learning rule. The key components are: (1) forward pass storing eligibility traces (h_prev and dtanh), (2) compute output error delta = dL/dh_T using W_out^T, (3) credit phase: propagate c_t = alpha * W_rec * c_{t+1} backward through time using W_rec (NOT transposed), (4) weight update: Delta_W = -lr * sum_t outer(c_t, e_t). I also need to handle the output layer gradient computation. I'll implement and test with a quick sanity check.", "action": "import sys\nimport os\n\n# Ensure torch is available\nanaconda_site_packages = \"/shared/utils.x86_64/anaconda3-2024.10/lib/python3.12/site-packages\"\nif anaconda_site_packages not in sys.path:\n    sys.path.insert(0, anaconda_site_packages)\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.optim as optim\nimport numpy as np\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')\nprint(f\"Device: {device}\")\n\n# ============================================================\n# COPY TASK + RNN (re-define for completeness)\n# ============================================================\ndef generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len=8, delay=10, device='cpu'):\n    total_len = seq_len + delay + seq_len\n    inputs = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len + 1, device=device)\n    targets = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len, device=device)\n    sequence = torch.randint(0, 2, (batch_size, seq_len), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    for t in range(seq_len):\n        inputs[t, :, :seq_len] = sequence\n    inputs[seq_len + delay:, :, seq_len] = 1.0\n    targets[seq_len + delay:, :, :] = sequence.unsqueeze(0).expand(seq_len, -1, -1)\n    mask = torch.zeros(total_len, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)\n    mask[seq_len + delay:] = True\n    return inputs, targets, mask\n\nclass VanillaRNN(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42):\n        super().__init__()\n        torch.manual_seed(seed)\n        self.input_dim = input_dim\n        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim\n        self.output_dim = output_dim\n        self.W_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.W_in = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, input_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.b_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(hidden_dim))\n        self.W_out = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(output_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.b_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(output_dim))\n        self.hiddens = []\n        self.elig_traces = []\n        self.outputs_list = []\n\n    def reset_storage(self):\n        self.hiddens = []\n        self.elig_traces = []\n        self.outputs_list = []\n\n    def forward(self, inputs, h0=None, store_activations=True):\n        T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape\n        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, self.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device) if h0 is None else h0\n        if store_activations:\n            self.reset_storage()\n        outputs = []\n        for t in range(T):\n            x_t = inputs[t]\n            a_t = h_prev @ self.W_rec.t() + x_t @ self.W_in.t() + self.b_rec\n            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)\n            y_t = h_t @ self.W_out.t() + self.b_out\n            if store_activations:\n                dtanh = 1.0 - h_t.detach() ** 2\n                self.hiddens.append(h_t.detach().clone())\n                self.elig_traces.append({\n                    'h_prev': h_prev.detach().clone(),\n                    'dtanh': dtanh.clone(),\n                    'x_t': x_t.detach().clone(),\n                })\n                self.outputs_list.append(y_t.detach().clone())\n            outputs.append(y_t)\n            h_prev = h_t\n        return torch.stack(outputs, dim=0), h_prev\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        return {k: v.data.clone() for k, v in [\n            ('W_rec', self.W_rec), ('W_in', self.W_in), ('b_rec', self.b_rec),\n            ('W_out', self.W_out), ('b_out', self.b_out)]}\n\n    def set_params_from_dict(self, d):\n        for k, v in d.items():\n            getattr(self, k).data.copy_(v)\n\ndef compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask):\n    preds = outputs[mask]\n    tgts = targets[mask]\n    loss = torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(preds, tgts)\n    acc = ((preds.detach() > 0.5).float() == tgts).float().mean().item()\n    return loss, acc\n\n# ============================================================\n# OPCA LEARNING RULE\n# ============================================================\ndef opca_update(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, alpha=0.9, \n                use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment) weight update.\n    \n    Args:\n        rnn: VanillaRNN instance\n        inputs: (T, batch, input_dim)\n        targets: (T, batch, output_dim)\n        mask: (T,) boolean - True at output timesteps\n        lr: learning rate\n        alpha: damping factor for credit propagation (< 1)\n        use_W_for_credit: if True, use W_rec for credit propagation (OPCA)\n                          if False, use B_matrix for credit propagation (FA)\n        B_matrix: fixed random matrix for FA (only used if use_W_for_credit=False)\n    \n    Returns:\n        loss: scalar loss\n        acc: bit accuracy\n    \"\"\"\n    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape\n    hidden_dim = rnn.hidden_dim\n    output_dim = rnn.output_dim\n    input_dim = rnn.input_dim\n    \n    # === FORWARD PHASE ===\n    # Run forward pass, storing eligibility traces\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n        hiddens = []\n        elig_traces = []\n        outputs_list = []\n        \n        for t in range(T):\n            x_t = inputs[t]\n            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec\n            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)\n            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out\n            \n            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t ** 2  # (batch, hidden)\n            \n            hiddens.append(h_t)\n            elig_traces.append({\n                'h_prev': h_prev.clone(),  # (batch, hidden)\n                'dtanh': dtanh.clone(),    # (batch, hidden)\n                'x_t': x_t.clone(),       # (batch, input_dim)\n            })\n            outputs_list.append(y_t)\n            h_prev = h_t\n        \n        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)  # (T, batch, output_dim)\n    \n    # === COMPUTE LOSS ===\n    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)\n    \n    # === OUTPUT ERROR ===\n    # delta_output = dL/dy at each timestep (2*(y-target)/n for MSE)\n    # Only non-zero at output timesteps\n    delta_output = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, output_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    n_output = mask.sum().item() * batch_size\n    # MSE gradient: dL/dy = 2*(y-target)/N, but we use factor 2/n\n    delta_output[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / (n_output * output_dim)\n    \n    # === INITIALIZE CREDIT SIGNAL ===\n    # delta_h_T = W_out^T * delta_output_T\n    # Propagate through output layer to get initial credit in hidden space\n    # For each timestep in output phase, we have a contribution to delta_h\n    delta_h = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    for t in range(T):\n        if mask[t]:\n            # dL/dh_t from output layer = delta_output_t @ W_out\n            delta_h[t] = delta_output[t] @ rnn.W_out  # (batch, hidden)\n    \n    # === CREDIT PHASE ===\n    # c_t = alpha * BackwardMatrix * c_{t+1} + delta_h_t\n    # where BackwardMatrix = W_rec (OPCA) or B (FA)\n    # Note: \"backward through time\" means c_{t} gets credit from c_{t+1}\n    \n    if use_W_for_credit:\n        B = rnn.W_rec.data  # Use W itself (OPCA)\n    else:\n        B = B_matrix  # Use random fixed matrix (FA)\n    \n    # Initialize credit signals (propagate backward through time)\n    credits = [None] * T\n    c_next = torch.zeros(batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    \n    for t in reversed(range(T)):\n        # Credit = contribution from future credits + current output error\n        # c_t = alpha * B * c_{t+1} + delta_h_t\n        # (B is hidden x hidden, c_{t+1} is batch x hidden)\n        # alpha * c_{t+1} @ B^T would be: batch x hidden @ hidden x hidden\n        # But OPCA uses W (not W^T): c_{t+1} @ W^T = c_{t+1} @ W.t()\n        # This is the KEY difference: we use W not W^T\n        c_t = alpha * (c_next @ B.t()) + delta_h[t]\n        # Modulate by dtanh (elementwise, credit signal * derivative)\n        c_t = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']\n        credits[t] = c_t\n        c_next = c_t\n    \n    # === WEIGHT UPDATE ===\n    # Delta_W_rec = -lr * sum_t mean_batch(c_t outer h_prev_t)\n    # = -lr * sum_t (c_t^T @ h_prev_t) / batch\n    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)\n    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)\n    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)\n    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)\n    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)\n    \n    for t in range(T):\n        c_t = credits[t]  # (batch, hidden)\n        h_prev_t = elig_traces[t]['h_prev']  # (batch, hidden)\n        x_t = elig_traces[t]['x_t']  # (batch, input_dim)\n        \n        # dW_rec += c_t^T @ h_prev / batch = (hidden, hidden)\n        dW_rec += c_t.t() @ h_prev_t / batch_size\n        \n        # dW_in += c_t^T @ x_t / batch = (hidden, input_dim)\n        dW_in += c_t.t() @ x_t / batch_size\n        \n        # db_rec += mean(c_t, dim=0)\n        db_rec += c_t.mean(dim=0)\n    \n    # Output layer: standard gradient\n    for t in range(T):\n        if mask[t]:\n            d_out = delta_output[t]  # (batch, output_dim)\n            h_t = hiddens[t]  # (batch, hidden)\n            dW_out += d_out.t() @ h_t / batch_size\n            db_out += d_out.mean(dim=0)\n    \n    # Apply updates with SGD\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * dW_rec\n        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * dW_in\n        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * db_rec\n        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * dW_out\n        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * db_out\n    \n    return loss.item(), acc\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# OPCA TRAINING FUNCTION\n# ============================================================\ndef train_opca(hidden_dim=128, lr=0.001, n_iter=5000, batch_size=32,\n               seq_len=8, delay=10, seed=42, device='cpu', log_every=50,\n               alpha=0.9, use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None):\n    \"\"\"Train RNN using OPCA learning rule.\"\"\"\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\n    input_dim = seq_len + 1\n    output_dim = seq_len\n    \n    rnn = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=seed).to(device)\n    \n    # If FA, create fixed random B matrix\n    if not use_W_for_credit and B_matrix is None:\n        B_matrix = torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, device=device) * 0.1\n    \n    loss_curve = []\n    acc_curve = []\n    \n    for iteration in range(n_iter):\n        inputs, targets, mask = generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len, delay, device=device)\n        \n        loss_val, acc_val = opca_update(\n            rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr=lr, alpha=alpha,\n            use_W_for_credit=use_W_for_credit, B_matrix=B_matrix\n        )\n        \n        if iteration % log_every == 0:\n            loss_curve.append(loss_val)\n            acc_curve.append(acc_val)\n    \n    return rnn, loss_curve, acc_curve\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# QUICK SANITY CHECK: OPCA\n# ============================================================\nprint(\"\\n=== OPCA Sanity Check ===\")\nprint(\"Training OPCA for 500 iterations (quick check)...\")\n\nrnn_opca, loss_curve_opca, acc_curve_opca = train_opca(\n    hidden_dim=128, lr=0.005, n_iter=500, batch_size=32,\n    seq_len=8, delay=10, seed=42, device=str(device), log_every=50,\n    alpha=0.9, use_W_for_credit=True\n)\n\nprint(f\"\\nOPCA Loss curve (every 50 iter):\")\nfor i, (l, a) in enumerate(zip(loss_curve_opca, acc_curve_opca)):\n    print(f\"  iter {i*50:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nOPCA initial loss: {loss_curve_opca[0]:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"OPCA final loss (500 iter): {loss_curve_opca[-1]:.4f}\")\nprint(f\"OPCA final accuracy: {acc_curve_opca[-1]:.3f}\")\n\nif loss_curve_opca[-1] < loss_curve_opca[0]:\n    print(\" OPCA is learning (loss decreased)\")\nelse:\n    print(\" WARNING: OPCA loss did not decrease!\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== OPCA Implementation: COMPLETE ===\")"}

import sys
import os

# Ensure torch is available
anaconda_site_packages = "/shared/utils.x86_64/anaconda3-2024.10/lib/python3.12/site-packages"
if anaconda_site_packages not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, anaconda_site_packages)

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
import numpy as np

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
print(f"Device: {device}")

# ============================================================
# COPY TASK + RNN (re-define for completeness)
# ============================================================
def generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len=8, delay=10, device='cpu'):
    total_len = seq_len + delay + seq_len
    inputs = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len + 1, device=device)
    targets = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len, device=device)
    sequence = torch.randint(0, 2, (batch_size, seq_len), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
    for t in range(seq_len):
        inputs[t, :, :seq_len] = sequence
    inputs[seq_len + delay:, :, seq_len] = 1.0
    targets[seq_len + delay:, :, :] = sequence.unsqueeze(0).expand(seq_len, -1, -1)
    mask = torch.zeros(total_len, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
    mask[seq_len + delay:] = True
    return inputs, targets, mask

class VanillaRNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42):
        super().__init__()
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        self.input_dim = input_dim
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
        self.output_dim = output_dim
        self.W_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.W_in = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, input_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(hidden_dim))
        self.W_out = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(output_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(output_dim))
        self.hiddens = []
        self.elig_traces = []
        self.outputs_list = []

    def reset_storage(self):
        self.hiddens = []
        self.elig_traces = []
        self.outputs_list = []

    def forward(self, inputs, h0=None, store_activations=True):
        T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, self.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device) if h0 is None else h0
        if store_activations:
            self.reset_storage()
        outputs = []
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ self.W_rec.t() + x_t @ self.W_in.t() + self.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ self.W_out.t() + self.b_out
            if store_activations:
                dtanh = 1.0 - h_t.detach() ** 2
                self.hiddens.append(h_t.detach().clone())
                self.elig_traces.append({
                    'h_prev': h_prev.detach().clone(),
                    'dtanh': dtanh.clone(),
                    'x_t': x_t.detach().clone(),
                })
                self.outputs_list.append(y_t.detach().clone())
            outputs.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        return torch.stack(outputs, dim=0), h_prev

    def get_params_dict(self):
        return {k: v.data.clone() for k, v in [
            ('W_rec', self.W_rec), ('W_in', self.W_in), ('b_rec', self.b_rec),
            ('W_out', self.W_out), ('b_out', self.b_out)]}

    def set_params_from_dict(self, d):
        for k, v in d.items():
            getattr(self, k).data.copy_(v)

def compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask):
    preds = outputs[mask]
    tgts = targets[mask]
    loss = torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(preds, tgts)
    acc = ((preds.detach() > 0.5).float() == tgts).float().mean().item()
    return loss, acc

# ============================================================
# OPCA LEARNING RULE
# ============================================================
def opca_update(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, alpha=0.9, 
                use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None):
    """
    OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment) weight update.
    
    Args:
        rnn: VanillaRNN instance
        inputs: (T, batch, input_dim)
        targets: (T, batch, output_dim)
        mask: (T,) boolean - True at output timesteps
        lr: learning rate
        alpha: damping factor for credit propagation (< 1)
        use_W_for_credit: if True, use W_rec for credit propagation (OPCA)
                          if False, use B_matrix for credit propagation (FA)
        B_matrix: fixed random matrix for FA (only used if use_W_for_credit=False)
    
    Returns:
        loss: scalar loss
        acc: bit accuracy
    """
    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
    hidden_dim = rnn.hidden_dim
    output_dim = rnn.output_dim
    input_dim = rnn.input_dim
    
    # === FORWARD PHASE ===
    # Run forward pass, storing eligibility traces
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
        hiddens = []
        elig_traces = []
        outputs_list = []
        
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out
            
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t ** 2  # (batch, hidden)
            
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            elig_traces.append({
                'h_prev': h_prev.clone(),  # (batch, hidden)
                'dtanh': dtanh.clone(),    # (batch, hidden)
                'x_t': x_t.clone(),       # (batch, input_dim)
            })
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)  # (T, batch, output_dim)
    
    # === COMPUTE LOSS ===
    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)
    
    # === OUTPUT ERROR ===
    # delta_output = dL/dy at each timestep (2*(y-target)/n for MSE)
    # Only non-zero at output timesteps
    delta_output = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, output_dim, device=inputs.device)
    n_output = mask.sum().item() * batch_size
    # MSE gradient: dL/dy = 2*(y-target)/N, but we use factor 2/n
    delta_output[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / (n_output * output_dim)
    
    # === INITIALIZE CREDIT SIGNAL ===
    # delta_h_T = W_out^T * delta_output_T
    # Propagate through output layer to get initial credit in hidden space
    # For each timestep in output phase, we have a contribution to delta_h
    delta_h = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            # dL/dh_t from output layer = delta_output_t @ W_out
            delta_h[t] = delta_output[t] @ rnn.W_out  # (batch, hidden)
    
    # === CREDIT PHASE ===
    # c_t = alpha * BackwardMatrix * c_{t+1} + delta_h_t
    # where BackwardMatrix = W_rec (OPCA) or B (FA)
    # Note: "backward through time" means c_{t} gets credit from c_{t+1}
    
    if use_W_for_credit:
        B = rnn.W_rec.data  # Use W itself (OPCA)
    else:
        B = B_matrix  # Use random fixed matrix (FA)
    
    # Initialize credit signals (propagate backward through time)
    credits = [None] * T
    c_next = torch.zeros(batch_size, hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Credit = contribution from future credits + current output error
        # c_t = alpha * B * c_{t+1} + delta_h_t
        # (B is hidden x hidden, c_{t+1} is batch x hidden)
        # alpha * c_{t+1} @ B^T would be: batch x hidden @ hidden x hidden
        # But OPCA uses W (not W^T): c_{t+1} @ W^T = c_{t+1} @ W.t()
        # This is the KEY difference: we use W not W^T
        c_t = alpha * (c_next @ B.t()) + delta_h[t]
        # Modulate by dtanh (elementwise, credit signal * derivative)
        c_t = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']
        credits[t] = c_t
        c_next = c_t
    
    # === WEIGHT UPDATE ===
    # Delta_W_rec = -lr * sum_t mean_batch(c_t outer h_prev_t)
    # = -lr * sum_t (c_t^T @ h_prev_t) / batch
    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)
    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)
    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)
    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)
    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)
    
    for t in range(T):
        c_t = credits[t]  # (batch, hidden)
        h_prev_t = elig_traces[t]['h_prev']  # (batch, hidden)
        x_t = elig_traces[t]['x_t']  # (batch, input_dim)
        
        # dW_rec += c_t^T @ h_prev / batch = (hidden, hidden)
        dW_rec += c_t.t() @ h_prev_t / batch_size
        
        # dW_in += c_t^T @ x_t / batch = (hidden, input_dim)
        dW_in += c_t.t() @ x_t / batch_size
        
        # db_rec += mean(c_t, dim=0)
        db_rec += c_t.mean(dim=0)
    
    # Output layer: standard gradient
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            d_out = delta_output[t]  # (batch, output_dim)
            h_t = hiddens[t]  # (batch, hidden)
            dW_out += d_out.t() @ h_t / batch_size
            db_out += d_out.mean(dim=0)
    
    # Apply updates with SGD
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * dW_rec
        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * dW_in
        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * db_rec
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * dW_out
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * db_out
    
    return loss.item(), acc


# ============================================================
# OPCA TRAINING FUNCTION
# ============================================================
def train_opca(hidden_dim=128, lr=0.001, n_iter=5000, batch_size=32,
               seq_len=8, delay=10, seed=42, device='cpu', log_every=50,
               alpha=0.9, use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None):
    """Train RNN using OPCA learning rule."""
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    input_dim = seq_len + 1
    output_dim = seq_len
    
    rnn = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=seed).to(device)
    
    # If FA, create fixed random B matrix
    if not use_W_for_credit and B_matrix is None:
        B_matrix = torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, device=device) * 0.1
    
    loss_curve = []
    acc_curve = []
    
    for iteration in range(n_iter):
        inputs, targets, mask = generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len, delay, device=device)
        
        loss_val, acc_val = opca_update(
            rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr=lr, alpha=alpha,
            use_W_for_credit=use_W_for_credit, B_matrix=B_matrix
        )
        
        if iteration % log_every == 0:
            loss_curve.append(loss_val)
            acc_curve.append(acc_val)
    
    return rnn, loss_curve, acc_curve


# ============================================================
# QUICK SANITY CHECK: OPCA
# ============================================================
print("\n=== OPCA Sanity Check ===")
print("Training OPCA for 500 iterations (quick check)...")

rnn_opca, loss_curve_opca, acc_curve_opca = train_opca(
    hidden_dim=128, lr=0.005, n_iter=500, batch_size=32,
    seq_len=8, delay=10, seed=42, device=str(device), log_every=50,
    alpha=0.9, use_W_for_credit=True
)

print(f"\nOPCA Loss curve (every 50 iter):")
for i, (l, a) in enumerate(zip(loss_curve_opca, acc_curve_opca)):
    print(f"  iter {i*50:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}")

print(f"\nOPCA initial loss: {loss_curve_opca[0]:.4f}")
print(f"OPCA final loss (500 iter): {loss_curve_opca[-1]:.4f}")
print(f"OPCA final accuracy: {acc_curve_opca[-1]:.3f}")

if loss_curve_opca[-1] < loss_curve_opca[0]:
    print(" OPCA is learning (loss decreased)")
else:
    print(" WARNING: OPCA loss did not decrease!")

print("\n=== OPCA Implementation: COMPLETE ===")

            db_out += d_out.mean(dim=0)

    with torch.no_grad():

        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * db_rec
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * dW_out
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * db_out
    
    return loss.item(), acc

               seq_len=8, delay=10, seed=42, device='cpu', log_every=50,

            rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr=lr, alpha=alpha,
            use_W_for_credit=use_W_for_credit, B_matrix=B_matrix
        )
        
        if iteration % log_every == 0:
            loss_curve.append(loss_val)
            acc_curve.append(acc_val)
    
    return rnn, loss_curve, acc_curve

Top-Level Task: Implement and test the OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment) learning rule for recurrent neural networks.

ALGORITHM DESCRIPTION:
OPCA is a biologically plausible learning rule that uses oscillatory phase dynamics to multiplex forward computation and credit assignment through the same weights:

1. Forward phase: h_t = tanh(W * h_{t-1} + U * x_t), store eligibility trace e_t^{ij} = h_{t-1,j} * tanh'(a_{t,i})
2. Output error: delta_T = dL/dh_T at final step
3. Credit phase: c_t = alpha * W * c_{t+1} (use W itself, not W^T, with damping factor alpha < 1)
   Initialize c_T = delta_T. Propagate backward through time using W (not transposed).
4. Weight update: Delta_W = -lr * sum_t (c_t outer_product e_t), Delta_U similarly

This is like feedback alignment but using the forward weights W instead of random B for the backward pass.

EXPERIMENT:
- Task: Copy task — input 8 binary values, wait 10 blank steps, then reproduce the 8 values
- Architecture: Vanilla RNN, hidden size 128
- Compare 4 methods: (a) BPTT (gold standard), (b) OPCA (our method, uses W for backward), (c) Feedback Alignment (uses random fixed B for backward), (d) RFLO-like (uses random feedback + local online)
- Training: 5000 iterations, batch size 32, learning rate search over {0.001, 0.005, 0.01}
- Metrics: Final MSE loss, convergence speed (iterations to reach 0.01 loss), bit accuracy

Also test a variant: OPCA with a learned scaling factor (alpha) for the credit propagation damping.

OUTPUT: Save results as a JSON file with loss curves and final metrics for each method.

ENVIRONMENT: Use conda environment panda. Install any needed packages with pip if missing.
Current Plan:
   1. Set up the environment: verify PyTorch is available in the panda conda environment, install any missing packages, and set up the output directory structure.
   2. Implement the copy task data generator: create a function that generates batches of copy task sequences (8 binary values, 10 blank steps delay, then reproduce the 8 values), returning input/target tensors.
   3. Implement the vanilla RNN architecture with separate modules for each learning method, including forward pass that stores intermediate activations (pre-activations, hidden states) needed for custom learning rules.
   4. Implement BPTT (standard backpropagation through time) as the gold standard baseline using PyTorch autograd.
   5. Implement OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment): forward phase with eligibility trace storage, credit phase propagating backward using W (not W^T) with damping factor alpha, and weight update rule.
   6. Implement Feedback Alignment: same structure as OPCA but uses a fixed random matrix B instead of W for the credit propagation backward pass.
   7. Implement RFLO-like learning rule: random feedback with local online updates (credit signal computed using random feedback weights, weights updated online at each timestep).
   8. Implement OPCA-learned-alpha variant: same as OPCA but with alpha as a learnable parameter that is optimized alongside the weights.
   9. Run the learning rate search and training loop for all 5 methods (BPTT, OPCA, FA, RFLO, OPCA-alpha) across learning rates {0.001, 0.005, 0.01}, training for 5000 iterations with batch size 32, recording loss curves every 50 iterations.
   10. Compute final metrics for each method: final MSE loss, convergence speed (iterations to reach loss 0.01), and bit accuracy on a held-out test set.
   11. Save all results (loss curves, final metrics, hyperparameter configurations) to a JSON file, then print a summary table comparing all methods.
   12. Write the final research report summarizing the methods, experimental setup, results, and conclusions about OPCA's performance relative to baselines.

seq_len=8, delay=10, device='cpu'):\n    total_len = seq_len + delay + seq_len\n    inputs = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len + 1, device=device)\n    targets = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len, device=device)\n    sequence = torch.randint(0, 2, (batch_size, seq_len), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\n    for t in range(seq_len):\n        inputs[t, :, :seq_len] = super().__init__()\n        torch.manual_seed(seed)\n        self.input_dim = input_dim\n        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim\n        self.output_dim = output_dim\n        self.W_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.W_in = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, input_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.b_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(hidden_dim))\n        self.W_out = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(output_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)\n        self.b_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(output_dim))\n\n    def get_params_dict(self):\n        return {k: getattr(self, k).data.clone() for k in ['W_rec','W_in','b_rec','W_out','b_out']}\n\n    def set_params_from_dict(self, d):\n        for k, v in d.items(): getattr(self, k).data.copy_(v)\n\ndef compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask):\n    preds = outputs[mask]\n    tgts = targets[mask]\n    loss = torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(preds, tgts)\n    acc = ((preds.detach() > 0.5).float() == tgts).float().mean().item()\n    return loss, acc\n\n# ============================================================\n# MANUAL BPTT (for verification)\n# ============================================================\ndef bptt_manual_update(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, clip_norm=1.0):\n    \"\"\"Manual BPTT using exact gradient computation (for verification).\"\"\"\n    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape\n    \n    with torch.no_grad():\n        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n        hiddens = []\n        pre_acts = []\n        elig_traces = []\n        outputs_list = []\n        \n        for t in range(T):\n            x_t = inputs[t]\n            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec\n            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)\n            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out\n            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2\n            hiddens.append(h_t)\n            pre_acts.append(a_t)\n            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t})\n            outputs_list.append(y_t)\n            h_prev = h_t\n        \n        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)\n    \n    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)\n    \n    # Compute gradients manually (exact BPTT)\n    # dL/dy_t = (2/n) * (y_t - target_t) for MSE\n    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim\n    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total\n    \n    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)\n    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)\n    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)\n    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)\n    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)\n    \n    # Output layer gradients\n    for t in range(T):\n        if mask[t]:\n            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size\n            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)\n    \n    # Backprop through time\n    delta_h = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    \n    for t in reversed(range(T)):\n        # Gradient from output\n        if mask[t]:\n            delta_h += delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out  # dL/dh from output layer\n        \n        # Gradient of tanh\n        delta_a = delta_h * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']  # elementwise\n        \n        # Weight gradients\n        dW_rec += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size\n        dW_in += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size\n        db_rec += delta_a.mean(0)\n        \n        # Backprop to previous hidden state (USES W^T)\n        delta_h = delta_a @ rnn.W_rec  # batch x hidden (uses W not W^T in this form)\n        # Note: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + ...), so dL/dh_{t-1} = delta_a @ W_rec\n        # (because d(h_{t-1} @ W^T)/dh_{t-1} = W^T^T = W)\n    \n    # Apply gradient clipping and update\n    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]\n    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5\n    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:\n        scale = clip_norm / total_norm\n        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]\n    \n    with torch.no_grad():\n        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * grads[0]\n        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * grads[1]\n        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * grads[2]\n        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * grads[3]\n        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * grads[4]\n    \n    return loss.item(), acc\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# FIXED OPCA LEARNING RULE\n# ============================================================\ndef opca_update_v2(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, alpha=0.9,\n                   use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None, clip_norm=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    OPCA v2: Fixed credit propagation.\n    \n    Key fix: OPCA uses W (not W^T) for credit propagation.\n    In batch form:\n      - Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + x_t @ U^T + b)\n      - BPTT backward: delta_{t-1} = (delta_t * dtanh_t) @ W_rec  [uses W, which is W^T^T]\n      - OPCA backward: credit_{t-1} = alpha * (credit_t * dtanh_t) @ B  [uses B without transpose]\n    \n    When B = W_rec: OPCA backward is same as BPTT (because @W = @W^T in this notation).\n    When B = random: this is feedback alignment.\n    \n    The true OPCA difference is that B is W (not W^T), but since the forward\n    already involves W^T in batch form, using W in backward (OPCA) is equivalent\n    to using W^T in the mathematical formulation.\n    \n    Actually the TASK DESCRIPTION says: c_t = alpha * W * c_{t+1}\n    where W is applied as a matrix multiply (W as left operand).\n    In batch form: c_{t+1} is (batch x hidden), W is (hidden x hidden)\n    So W * c_{t+1} = W @ c_{t+1} where c_{t+1} is treated as column vectors.\n    In batch form this is: c_{t+1} @ W^T\n    This is the SAME as BPTT (which also effectively uses W).\n    \n    The DISTINCTION: FA uses random B^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ B^T\n    OPCA uses W^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ W (the forward weight WITHOUT transpose)\n    \n    So the key difference from BPTT is:\n    - BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec (correct gradient, W_rec IS the weight matrix)\n    - OPCA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ W_rec (same as BPTT in batch form!)\n    - FA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ B (random B)\n    \n    Wait - let's re-read: \"use W itself, not W^T\"\n    If forward is: h_t = tanh(W_rec @ h_{t-1} + ...) [column vectors]\n    Then BPTT backprop: delta_{t-1} = W_rec^T @ delta_t\n    OPCA: c_{t-1} = W_rec @ c_t (no transpose!) <- this IS different from BPTT\n    \n    In batch form where h is (batch x hidden) row vectors:\n    Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W_rec^T + ...)\n    BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec  (=W_rec^T^T = W_rec in column notation)\n    OPCA: c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ W_rec^T  (=W_rec in column notation, NO transpose)\n    FA:   c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ B^T  (random B)\n    \"\"\"\n    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape\n    \n    with torch.no_grad():\n        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n        hiddens = []\n        elig_traces = []\n        outputs_list = []\n        \n        for t in range(T):\n            x_t = inputs[t]\n            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec\n            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)\n            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out\n            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2\n            hiddens.append(h_t)\n            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t.clone()})\n            outputs_list.append(y_t)\n            h_prev = h_t\n        \n        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)\n    \n    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)\n    \n    # Output error\n    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim\n    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total\n    \n    # Map output error to hidden space\n    delta_h_from_output = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    for t in range(T):\n        if mask[t]:\n            delta_h_from_output[t] = delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out\n    \n    # Choose backward matrix\n    if use_W_for_credit:\n        B = rnn.W_rec.data  # OPCA: use W (no transpose in column form = transpose in batch form)\n    else:\n        B = B_matrix  # FA: use random B\n    \n    # Credit propagation backward through time\n    # OPCA: c_t = alpha * W_rec @ c_{t+1} (column vector form)\n    # In batch form: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W_rec.t() <- uses W_rec.t() in batch form\n    # BPTT: delta_t = delta_{t+1} @ W_rec (batch form) = W_rec.t() @ delta_{t+1} (column form)\n    # So OPCA (c @ W.t()) is DIFFERENT from BPTT (delta @ W)\n    credits = [None] * T\n    c_next = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)\n    \n    for t in reversed(range(T)):\n        # Add output error at this timestep\n        c_in = delta_h_from_output[t].clone()\n        \n        # OPCA: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W.t() + delta_h_t (W not transposed in col form)\n        # BPTT: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W + delta_h_t (W transposed in col form)\n        if use_W_for_credit:\n            # OPCA: use W_rec.t() in batch form (= W_rec in column form, no transpose)\n            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # This is OPCA (W in col form)\n        else:\n            # FA: use B.t() in batch form\n            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # Same formula, different B\n        \n        c_t = c_t_from_future + c_in\n        # Apply tanh derivative\n        c_t_mod = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']\n        credits[t] = c_t_mod\n        c_next = c_t  # Pass unmodulated credit to previous step (matches BPTT)\n    \n    # Weight updates\n    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)\n    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)\n    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)\n    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)\n    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)\n    \n    for t in range(T):\n        c_t = credits[t]\n        dW_rec += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size\n        dW_in += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size\n        db_rec += c_t.mean(0)\n    \n    for t in range(T):\n        if mask[t]:\n            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size\n            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)\n    \n    # Gradient clipping\n    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]\n    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5\n    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:\n        scale = clip_norm / total_norm\n        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]\n    \n    with torch.no_grad():\n        rnn.W_rec.data -= ============================== Panda ==============================
Coding thoughts:
Thought: The key issue is: in OPCA, we use W (not W^T) for credit propagation. In batch form, if h_t = h_{t-1} @ W^T (forward), then for backward with W (not W^T), we should compute c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W (without transpose). This is what makes OPCA different from BPTT (which uses W^T). Also, I need to reconsider the gradient scaling - the current normalization may be too aggressive. Let me also try removing the dtanh modulation from the credit signal itself (apply it separately in the weight update), and adjust the learning rate scaling. I'll also verify by comparing against a 'BPTT-manual' implementation to make sure I have the gradient computation right.
Action (code):
import sys
import os

anaconda_site_packages = "/shared/utils.x86_64/anaconda3-2024.10/lib/python3.12/site-packages"
if anaconda_site_packages not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, anaconda_site_packages)

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
import numpy as np

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
print(f"Device: {device}")

# ============================================================
# COPY TASK + RNN (re-define)
# ============================================================
def generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len=8, delay=10, device='cpu'):
    total_len = seq_len + delay + seq_len
    inputs = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len + 1, device=device)
    targets = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len, device=device)
    sequence = torch.randint(0, 2, (batch_size, seq_len), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
    for t in range(seq_len):
        inputs[t, :, :seq_len] = sequence
    inputs[seq_len + delay:, :, seq_len] = 1.0
    targets[seq_len + delay:, :, :] = sequence.unsqueeze(0).expand(seq_len, -1, -1)
    mask = torch.zeros(total_len, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
    mask[seq_len + delay:] = True
    return inputs, targets, mask

class VanillaRNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42):
        super().__init__()
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        self.input_dim = input_dim
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
        self.output_dim = output_dim
        self.W_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.W_in = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, input_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(hidden_dim))
        self.W_out = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(output_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(output_dim))

    def get_params_dict(self):
        return {k: getattr(self, k).data.clone() for k in ['W_rec','W_in','b_rec','W_out','b_out']}

    def set_params_from_dict(self, d):
        for k, v in d.items(): getattr(self, k).data.copy_(v)

def compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask):
    preds = outputs[mask]
    tgts = targets[mask]
    loss = torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(preds, tgts)
    acc = ((preds.detach() > 0.5).float() == tgts).float().mean().item()
    return loss, acc

# ============================================================
# MANUAL BPTT (for verification)
# ============================================================
def bptt_manual_update(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, clip_norm=1.0):
    """Manual BPTT using exact gradient computation (for verification)."""
    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
        hiddens = []
        pre_acts = []
        elig_traces = []
        outputs_list = []
        
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            pre_acts.append(a_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)
    
    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)
    
    # Compute gradients manually (exact BPTT)
    # dL/dy_t = (2/n) * (y_t - target_t) for MSE
    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)
    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total
    
    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)
    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)
    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)
    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)
    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)
    
    # Output layer gradients
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size
            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)
    
    # Backprop through time
    delta_h = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Gradient from output
        if mask[t]:
            delta_h += delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out  # dL/dh from output layer
        
        # Gradient of tanh
        delta_a = delta_h * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']  # elementwise
        
        # Weight gradients
        dW_rec += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size
        dW_in += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size
        db_rec += delta_a.mean(0)
        
        # Backprop to previous hidden state (USES W^T)
        delta_h = delta_a @ rnn.W_rec  # batch x hidden (uses W not W^T in this form)
        # Note: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + ...), so dL/dh_{t-1} = delta_a @ W_rec
        # (because d(h_{t-1} @ W^T)/dh_{t-1} = W^T^T = W)
    
    # Apply gradient clipping and update
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:
        scale = clip_norm / total_norm
        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * grads[0]
        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * grads[1]
        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * grads[2]
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * grads[3]
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * grads[4]
    
    return loss.item(), acc


# ============================================================
# FIXED OPCA LEARNING RULE
# ============================================================
def opca_update_v2(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, alpha=0.9,
                   use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None, clip_norm=1.0):
    """
    OPCA v2: Fixed credit propagation.
    
    Key fix: OPCA uses W (not W^T) for credit propagation.
    In batch form:
      - Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + x_t @ U^T + b)
      - BPTT backward: delta_{t-1} = (delta_t * dtanh_t) @ W_rec  [uses W, which is W^T^T]
      - OPCA backward: credit_{t-1} = alpha * (credit_t * dtanh_t) @ B  [uses B without transpose]
    
    When B = W_rec: OPCA backward is same as BPTT (because @W = @W^T in this notation).
    When B = random: this is feedback alignment.
    
    The true OPCA difference is that B is W (not W^T), but since the forward
    already involves W^T in batch form, using W in backward (OPCA) is equivalent
    to using W^T in the mathematical formulation.
    
    Actually the TASK DESCRIPTION says: c_t = alpha * W * c_{t+1}
    where W is applied as a matrix multiply (W as left operand).
    In batch form: c_{t+1} is (batch x hidden), W is (hidden x hidden)
    So W * c_{t+1} = W @ c_{t+1} where c_{t+1} is treated as column vectors.
    In batch form this is: c_{t+1} @ W^T
    This is the SAME as BPTT (which also effectively uses W).
    
    The DISTINCTION: FA uses random B^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ B^T
    OPCA uses W^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ W (the forward weight WITHOUT transpose)
    
    So the key difference from BPTT is:
    - BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec (correct gradient, W_rec IS the weight matrix)
    - OPCA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ W_rec (same as BPTT in batch form!)
    - FA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ B (random B)
    
    Wait - let's re-read: "use W itself, not W^T"
    If forward is: h_t = tanh(W_rec @ h_{t-1} + ...) [column vectors]
    Then BPTT backprop: delta_{t-1} = W_rec^T @ delta_t
    OPCA: c_{t-1} = W_rec @ c_t (no transpose!) <- this IS different from BPTT
    
    In batch form where h is (batch x hidden) row vectors:
    Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W_rec^T + ...)
    BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec  (=W_rec^T^T = W_rec in column notation)
    OPCA: c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ W_rec^T  (=W_rec in column notation, NO transpose)
    FA:   c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ B^T  (random B)
    """
    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
        hiddens = []
        elig_traces = []
        outputs_list = []
        
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t.clone()})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)
    
    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)
    
    # Output error
    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)
    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total
    
    # Map output error to hidden space
    delta_h_from_output = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            delta_h_from_output[t] = delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out
    
    # Choose backward matrix
    if use_W_for_credit:
        B = rnn.W_rec.data  # OPCA: use W (no transpose in column form = transpose in batch form)
    else:
        B = B_matrix  # FA: use random B
    
    # Credit propagation backward through time
    # OPCA: c_t = alpha * W_rec @ c_{t+1} (column vector form)
    # In batch form: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W_rec.t() <- uses W_rec.t() in batch form
    # BPTT: delta_t = delta_{t+1} @ W_rec (batch form) = W_rec.t() @ delta_{t+1} (column form)
    # So OPCA (c @ W.t()) is DIFFERENT from BPTT (delta @ W)
    credits = [None] * T
    c_next = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Add output error at this timestep
        c_in = delta_h_from_output[t].clone()
        
        # OPCA: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W.t() + delta_h_t (W not transposed in col form)
        # BPTT: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W + delta_h_t (W transposed in col form)
        if use_W_for_credit:
            # OPCA: use W_rec.t() in batch form (= W_rec in column form, no transpose)
            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # This is OPCA (W in col form)
        else:
            # FA: use B.t() in batch form
            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # Same formula, different B
        
        c_t = c_t_from_future + c_in
        # Apply tanh derivative
        c_t_mod = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']
        credits[t] = c_t_mod
        c_next = c_t  # Pass unmodulated credit to previous step (matches BPTT)
    
    # Weight updates
    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)
    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)
    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)
    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)
    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)
    
    for t in range(T):
        c_t = credits[t]
        dW_rec += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size
        dW_in += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size
        db_rec += c_t.mean(0)
    
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size
            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)
    
    # Gradient clipping
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:
        scale = clip_norm / total_norm
        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * grads[0]
        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * grads[1]
        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * grads[2]
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * grads[3]
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * grads[4]
    
    return loss.item(), acc


# ============================================================
# VERIFY: Manual BPTT vs Autograd BPTT
# ============================================================
print("=== Verifying Manual BPTT ===")
torch.manual_seed(42)
input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim = 9, 128, 8

# Create identical RNNs
rnn_a = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)
rnn_b = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)

# Test with same batch
inputs_t, targets_t, mask_t = generate_copy_task_batch(32, device=str(device))

# Manual BPTT
loss_manual, acc_manual = bptt_manual_update(rnn_a, inputs_t, targets_t, mask_t, lr=0.005)
print(f"Manual BPTT: loss={loss_manual:.4f}, acc={acc_manual:.3f}")

# Autograd BPTT (for comparison)
rnn_b_optim = optim.Adam(rnn_b.parameters(), lr=0.005)
rnn_b_optim.zero_grad()
outputs_b = []
h_prev = torch.zeros(32, hidden_dim, device=device)
for t in range(inputs_t.shape[0]):
    x_t = inputs_t[t]
    a_t = h_prev @ rnn_b.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn_b.W_in.t() + rnn_b.b_rec
    h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
    y_t = h_t @ rnn_b.W_out.t() + rnn_b.b_out
    outputs_b.append(y_t)
    h_prev = h_t
outputs_b = torch.stack(outputs_b, 0)
loss_auto, acc_auto = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs_b, targets_t, mask_t)
loss_auto.backward()
rnn_b_optim.step()
print(f"Auto BPTT: loss={loss_auto.item():.4f}, acc={acc_auto:.3f}")

# ============================================================
# TRAIN Manual BPTT for 500 iter
# ============================================================
print("\n=== Manual BPTT 500 iter ===")
torch.manual_seed(42)
rnn_mbptt = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)
loss_mbptt = []
for i in range(500):
    inp, tgt, msk = generate_copy_task_batch(32, device=str(device))
    l, a = bptt_manual_update(rnn_mbptt, inp, tgt, msk, lr=0.005)
    if i % 50 == 0:
        loss_mbptt.append((i, l, a))
for i, l, a in loss_mbptt:
    print(f"  iter {i:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}")

# ============================================================
# TRAIN OPCA v2 for 500 iter
# ============================================================
print("\n=== OPCA v2 (W for backward) 500 iter ===")
torch.manual_seed(42)
rnn_opca2 = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)
loss_opca2 = []
for i in range(500):
    inp, tgt, msk = generate_copy_task_batch(32, device=str(device))
    l, a = opca_update_v2(rnn_opca2, inp, tgt, msk, lr=0.005, alpha=0.9, use_W_for_credit=True)
    if i % 50 == 0:
        loss_opca2.append((i, l, a))
for i, l, a in loss_opca2:
    print(f"  iter {i:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}")

# Try with OPCA using W without transpose (true difference from BPTT)
print("\n=== OPCA v2 - W without transpose (c @ W not c @ W.t()) ===")
torch.manual_seed(42)
rnn_opca3 = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)
loss_opca3 = []

for i in range(500):
    inp, tgt, msk = generate_copy_task_batch(32, device=str(device))
    T, bs, _ = inp.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
        hiddens, elig_traces, outputs_list = [], [], []
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inp[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn_opca3.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn_opca3.W_in.t() + rnn_opca3.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn_opca3.W_out.t() + rnn_opca3.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t.clone()})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, 0)
    
    loss_val, acc_val = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, tgt, msk)
    n_total = msk.sum().item() * bs * output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, bs, output_dim, device=inp.device)
    delta_y[msk] = 2.0 * (outputs[msk] - tgt[msk]) / n_total
    
    delta_h_out = torch.zeros(T, bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
    for t in range(T):
        if msk[t]:
            delta_h_out[t] = delta_y[t] @ rnn_opca3.W_out
    
    # TRUE OPCA: c_t = alpha * W_rec @ c_{t+1} (column form)
    # In batch form: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W_rec.t() <- W.t() in batch = W in column
    # This is what the description says: use W (not W^T)
    credits = [None] * T
    c_next = torch.zeros(bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Use W_rec WITHOUT transpose (c @ W, not c @ W^T)
        # In column form this is: W^T @ c = W_rec^T @ c_{t+1}
        c_t = alpha_val = 0.9
        c_t = 0.9 * (c_next @ rnn_opca3.W_rec) + delta_h_out[t]  # NO .t() = different from BPTT
        c_t = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']
        credits[t] = c_t
        c_next = c_t
    
    dW_rec = sum(credits[t].t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] for t in range(T)) / bs
    dW_in = sum(credits[t].t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] for t in range(T)) / bs
    db_rec = sum(credits[t].mean(0) for t in range(T))
    dW_out = sum(delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] for t in range(T) if msk[t]) / bs
    db_out = sum(delta_y[t].mean(0) for t in range(T) if msk[t])
    
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if total_norm > 1.0:
        grads = [g / total_norm for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn_opca3.W_rec.data -= 0.005 * grads[0]
        rnn_opca3.W_in.data -= 0.005 * grads[1]
        rnn_opca3.b_rec.data -= 0.005 * grads[2]
        rnn_opca3.W_out.data -= 0.005 * grads[3]
        rnn_opca3.b_out.data -= 0.005 * grads[4]
    
    if i % 50 == 0:
        loss_opca3.append((i, loss_val.item(), acc_val))

for i, l, a in loss_opca3:
    print(f"  iter {i:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}")

print("\n=== OPCA Debug Complete ===")

In [182]: def generate_copy_task_batch(batch_size, seq_len=8, delay=10, device='cpu'):
    total_len = seq_len + delay + seq_len
    inputs = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len + 1, device=device)
    targets = torch.zeros(total_len, batch_size, seq_len, device=device)
    sequence = torch.randint(0, 2, (batch_size, seq_len), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
    for t in range(seq_len):
        inputs[t, :, :seq_len] = sequence
    inputs[seq_len + delay:, :, seq_len] = 1.0
    targets[seq_len + delay:, :, :] = sequence.unsqueeze(0).expand(seq_len, -1, -1)
    mask = torch.zeros(total_len, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
    mask[seq_len + delay:] = True
    return inputs, targets, mask

In [183]: class VanillaRNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42):
        super().__init__()
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        self.input_dim = input_dim
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
        self.output_dim = output_dim
        self.W_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.W_in = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(hidden_dim, input_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_rec = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(hidden_dim))
        self.W_out = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(output_dim, hidden_dim) * 0.1)
        self.b_out = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(output_dim))

    def get_params_dict(self):
        return {k: getattr(self, k).data.clone() for k in ['W_rec','W_in','b_rec','W_out','b_out']}

    def set_params_from_dict(self, d):
        for k, v in d.items(): getattr(self, k).data.copy_(v)

In [184]: def compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask):
    preds = outputs[mask]
    tgts = targets[mask]
    loss = torch.nn.functional.mse_loss(preds, tgts)
    acc = ((preds.detach() > 0.5).float() == tgts).float().mean().item()
    return loss, acc

In [185]: def bptt_manual_update(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, clip_norm=1.0):
    """Manual BPTT using exact gradient computation (for verification)."""
    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
        hiddens = []
        pre_acts = []
        elig_traces = []
        outputs_list = []
        
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            pre_acts.append(a_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)
    
    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)
    
    # Compute gradients manually (exact BPTT)
    # dL/dy_t = (2/n) * (y_t - target_t) for MSE
    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)
    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total
    
    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)
    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)
    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)
    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)
    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)
    
    # Output layer gradients
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size
            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)
    
    # Backprop through time
    delta_h = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Gradient from output
        if mask[t]:
            delta_h += delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out  # dL/dh from output layer
        
        # Gradient of tanh
        delta_a = delta_h * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']  # elementwise
        
        # Weight gradients
        dW_rec += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size
        dW_in += delta_a.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size
        db_rec += delta_a.mean(0)
        
        # Backprop to previous hidden state (USES W^T)
        delta_h = delta_a @ rnn.W_rec  # batch x hidden (uses W not W^T in this form)
        # Note: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + ...), so dL/dh_{t-1} = delta_a @ W_rec
        # (because d(h_{t-1} @ W^T)/dh_{t-1} = W^T^T = W)
    
    # Apply gradient clipping and update
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:
        scale = clip_norm / total_norm
        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * grads[0]
        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * grads[1]
        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * grads[2]
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * grads[3]
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * grads[4]
    
    return loss.item(), acc

In [186]: def opca_update_v2(rnn, inputs, targets, mask, lr, alpha=0.9,
                   use_W_for_credit=True, B_matrix=None, clip_norm=1.0):
    """
    OPCA v2: Fixed credit propagation.
    
    Key fix: OPCA uses W (not W^T) for credit propagation.
    In batch form:
      - Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W^T + x_t @ U^T + b)
      - BPTT backward: delta_{t-1} = (delta_t * dtanh_t) @ W_rec  [uses W, which is W^T^T]
      - OPCA backward: credit_{t-1} = alpha * (credit_t * dtanh_t) @ B  [uses B without transpose]
    
    When B = W_rec: OPCA backward is same as BPTT (because @W = @W^T in this notation).
    When B = random: this is feedback alignment.
    
    The true OPCA difference is that B is W (not W^T), but since the forward
    already involves W^T in batch form, using W in backward (OPCA) is equivalent
    to using W^T in the mathematical formulation.
    
    Actually the TASK DESCRIPTION says: c_t = alpha * W * c_{t+1}
    where W is applied as a matrix multiply (W as left operand).
    In batch form: c_{t+1} is (batch x hidden), W is (hidden x hidden)
    So W * c_{t+1} = W @ c_{t+1} where c_{t+1} is treated as column vectors.
    In batch form this is: c_{t+1} @ W^T
    This is the SAME as BPTT (which also effectively uses W).
    
    The DISTINCTION: FA uses random B^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ B^T
    OPCA uses W^T in batch form = c_{t+1} @ W (the forward weight WITHOUT transpose)
    
    So the key difference from BPTT is:
    - BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec (correct gradient, W_rec IS the weight matrix)
    - OPCA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ W_rec (same as BPTT in batch form!)
    - FA: credit_{t-1} = alpha * credit_t @ B (random B)
    
    Wait - let's re-read: "use W itself, not W^T"
    If forward is: h_t = tanh(W_rec @ h_{t-1} + ...) [column vectors]
    Then BPTT backprop: delta_{t-1} = W_rec^T @ delta_t
    OPCA: c_{t-1} = W_rec @ c_t (no transpose!) <- this IS different from BPTT
    
    In batch form where h is (batch x hidden) row vectors:
    Forward: h_t = tanh(h_{t-1} @ W_rec^T + ...)
    BPTT: delta_{t-1} = delta_t @ W_rec  (=W_rec^T^T = W_rec in column notation)
    OPCA: c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ W_rec^T  (=W_rec in column notation, NO transpose)
    FA:   c_{t-1} = alpha * c_t @ B^T  (random B)
    """
    T, batch_size, _ = inputs.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
        hiddens = []
        elig_traces = []
        outputs_list = []
        
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inputs[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn.W_in.t() + rnn.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn.W_out.t() + rnn.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t.clone()})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, dim=0)
    
    loss, acc = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, targets, mask)
    
    # Output error
    n_total = mask.sum().item() * batch_size * rnn.output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.output_dim, device=inputs.device)
    delta_y[mask] = 2.0 * (outputs[mask] - targets[mask]) / n_total
    
    # Map output error to hidden space
    delta_h_from_output = torch.zeros(T, batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            delta_h_from_output[t] = delta_y[t] @ rnn.W_out
    
    # Choose backward matrix
    if use_W_for_credit:
        B = rnn.W_rec.data  # OPCA: use W (no transpose in column form = transpose in batch form)
    else:
        B = B_matrix  # FA: use random B
    
    # Credit propagation backward through time
    # OPCA: c_t = alpha * W_rec @ c_{t+1} (column vector form)
    # In batch form: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W_rec.t() <- uses W_rec.t() in batch form
    # BPTT: delta_t = delta_{t+1} @ W_rec (batch form) = W_rec.t() @ delta_{t+1} (column form)
    # So OPCA (c @ W.t()) is DIFFERENT from BPTT (delta @ W)
    credits = [None] * T
    c_next = torch.zeros(batch_size, rnn.hidden_dim, device=inputs.device)
    
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Add output error at this timestep
        c_in = delta_h_from_output[t].clone()
        
        # OPCA: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W.t() + delta_h_t (W not transposed in col form)
        # BPTT: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W + delta_h_t (W transposed in col form)
        if use_W_for_credit:
            # OPCA: use W_rec.t() in batch form (= W_rec in column form, no transpose)
            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # This is OPCA (W in col form)
        else:
            # FA: use B.t() in batch form
            c_t_from_future = alpha * (c_next @ B.t())  # Same formula, different B
        
        c_t = c_t_from_future + c_in
        # Apply tanh derivative
        c_t_mod = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']
        credits[t] = c_t_mod
        c_next = c_t  # Pass unmodulated credit to previous step (matches BPTT)
    
    # Weight updates
    dW_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_rec.data)
    dW_in = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_in.data)
    db_rec = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_rec.data)
    dW_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.W_out.data)
    db_out = torch.zeros_like(rnn.b_out.data)
    
    for t in range(T):
        c_t = credits[t]
        dW_rec += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] / batch_size
        dW_in += c_t.t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] / batch_size
        db_rec += c_t.mean(0)
    
    for t in range(T):
        if mask[t]:
            dW_out += delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] / batch_size
            db_out += delta_y[t].mean(0)
    
    # Gradient clipping
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if clip_norm > 0 and total_norm > clip_norm:
        scale = clip_norm / total_norm
        grads = [g * scale for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn.W_rec.data -= lr * grads[0]
        rnn.W_in.data -= lr * grads[1]
        rnn.b_rec.data -= lr * grads[2]
        rnn.W_out.data -= lr * grads[3]
        rnn.b_out.data -= lr * grads[4]
    
    return loss.item(), acc

In [211]: print("\n=== OPCA v2 (W for backward) 500 iter ===")

In [217]: print("\n=== OPCA v2 - W without transpose (c @ W not c @ W.t()) ===")

=== OPCA v2 - W without transpose (c @ W not c @ W.t()) ===

In [218]: torch.manual_seed(42)

In [219]: rnn_opca3 = VanillaRNN(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, seed=42).to(device)

In [220]: loss_opca3 = []

In [221]: for i in range(500):
    inp, tgt, msk = generate_copy_task_batch(32, device=str(device))
    T, bs, _ = inp.shape
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        h_prev = torch.zeros(bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
        hiddens, elig_traces, outputs_list = [], [], []
        for t in range(T):
            x_t = inp[t]
            a_t = h_prev @ rnn_opca3.W_rec.t() + x_t @ rnn_opca3.W_in.t() + rnn_opca3.b_rec
            h_t = torch.tanh(a_t)
            y_t = h_t @ rnn_opca3.W_out.t() + rnn_opca3.b_out
            dtanh = 1.0 - h_t**2
            hiddens.append(h_t)
            elig_traces.append({'h_prev': h_prev.clone(), 'dtanh': dtanh, 'x_t': x_t.clone()})
            outputs_list.append(y_t)
            h_prev = h_t
        outputs = torch.stack(outputs_list, 0)
    
    loss_val, acc_val = compute_loss_and_metrics(outputs, tgt, msk)
    n_total = msk.sum().item() * bs * output_dim
    delta_y = torch.zeros(T, bs, output_dim, device=inp.device)
    delta_y[msk] = 2.0 * (outputs[msk] - tgt[msk]) / n_total
    
    delta_h_out = torch.zeros(T, bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
    for t in range(T):
        if msk[t]:
            delta_h_out[t] = delta_y[t] @ rnn_opca3.W_out
    
    # TRUE OPCA: c_t = alpha * W_rec @ c_{t+1} (column form)
    # In batch form: c_t = alpha * c_{t+1} @ W_rec.t() <- W.t() in batch = W in column
    # This is what the description says: use W (not W^T)
    credits = [None] * T
    c_next = torch.zeros(bs, hidden_dim, device=inp.device)
    for t in reversed(range(T)):
        # Use W_rec WITHOUT transpose (c @ W, not c @ W^T)
        # In column form this is: W^T @ c = W_rec^T @ c_{t+1}
        c_t = alpha_val = 0.9
        c_t = 0.9 * (c_next @ rnn_opca3.W_rec) + delta_h_out[t]  # NO .t() = different from BPTT
        c_t = c_t * elig_traces[t]['dtanh']
        credits[t] = c_t
        c_next = c_t
    
    dW_rec = sum(credits[t].t() @ elig_traces[t]['h_prev'] for t in range(T)) / bs
    dW_in = sum(credits[t].t() @ elig_traces[t]['x_t'] for t in range(T)) / bs
    db_rec = sum(credits[t].mean(0) for t in range(T))
    dW_out = sum(delta_y[t].t() @ hiddens[t] for t in range(T) if msk[t]) / bs
    db_out = sum(delta_y[t].mean(0) for t in range(T) if msk[t])
    
    grads = [dW_rec, dW_in, db_rec, dW_out, db_out]
    total_norm = sum(g.norm()**2 for g in grads) ** 0.5
    if total_norm > 1.0:
        grads = [g / total_norm for g in grads]
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        rnn_opca3.W_rec.data -= 0.005 * grads[0]
        rnn_opca3.W_in.data -= 0.005 * grads[1]
        rnn_opca3.b_rec.data -= 0.005 * grads[2]
        rnn_opca3.W_out.data -= 0.005 * grads[3]
        rnn_opca3.b_out.data -= 0.005 * grads[4]
    
    if i % 50 == 0:
        loss_opca3.append((i, loss_val.item(), acc_val))

    print(f"  iter {i:4d}: loss={l:.4f}, acc={a:.3f}")

Top-Level Task: Implement and test the OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment) learning rule for recurrent neural networks.

ALGORITHM DESCRIPTION:
OPCA is a biologically plausible learning rule that uses oscillatory phase dynamics to multiplex forward computation and credit assignment through the same weights:

1. Forward phase: h_t = tanh(W * h_{t-1} + U * x_t), store eligibility trace e_t^{ij} = h_{t-1,j} * tanh'(a_{t,i})
2. Output error: delta_T = dL/dh_T at final step
3. Credit phase: c_t = alpha * W * c_{t+1} (use W itself, not W^T, with damping factor alpha < 1)
   Initialize c_T = delta_T. Propagate backward through time using W (not transposed).
4. Weight update: Delta_W = -lr * sum_t (c_t outer_product e_t), Delta_U similarly

This is like feedback alignment but using the forward weights W instead of random B for the backward pass.

EXPERIMENT:
- Task: Copy task — input 8 binary values, wait 10 blank steps, then reproduce the 8 values
- Architecture: Vanilla RNN, hidden size 128
- Compare 4 methods: (a) BPTT (gold standard), (b) OPCA (our method, uses W for backward), (c) Feedback Alignment (uses random fixed B for backward), (d) RFLO-like (uses random feedback + local online)
- Training: 5000 iterations, batch size 32, learning rate search over {0.001, 0.005, 0.01}
- Metrics: Final MSE loss, convergence speed (iterations to reach 0.01 loss), bit accuracy

Also test a variant: OPCA with a learned scaling factor (alpha) for the credit propagation damping.

OUTPUT: Save results as a JSON file with loss curves and final metrics for each method.

ENVIRONMENT: Use conda environment panda. Install any needed packages with pip if missing.
Current Plan:
   1. Set up the environment: verify PyTorch is available in the panda conda environment, install any missing packages, and set up the output directory structure.
   2. Implement the copy task data generator: create a function that generates batches of copy task sequences (8 binary values, 10 blank steps delay, then reproduce the 8 values), returning input/target tensors.
   3. Implement the vanilla RNN architecture with separate modules for each learning method, including forward pass that stores intermediate activations (pre-activations, hidden states) needed for custom learning rules.
   4. Implement BPTT (standard backpropagation through time) as the gold standard baseline using PyTorch autograd.
   5. Implement OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment): forward phase with eligibility trace storage, credit phase propagating backward using W (not W^T) with damping factor alpha, and weight update rule.
   6. Implement Feedback Alignment: same structure as OPCA but uses a fixed random matrix B instead of W for the credit propagation backward pass.
   7. Implement RFLO-like learning rule: random feedback with local online updates (credit signal computed using random feedback weights, weights updated online at each timestep).
   8. Implement OPCA-learned-alpha variant: same as OPCA but with alpha as a learnable parameter that is optimized alongside the weights.
   9. Run the learning rate search and training loop for all 5 methods (BPTT, OPCA, FA, RFLO, OPCA-alpha) across learning rates {0.001, 0.005, 0.01}, training for 5000 iterations with batch size 32, recording loss curves every 50 iterations.
   10. Compute final metrics for each method: final MSE loss, convergence speed (iterations to reach loss 0.01), and bit accuracy on a held-out test set.
   11. Save all results (loss curves, final metrics, hyperparameter configurations) to a JSON file, then print a summary table comparing all methods.
   12. Write the final research report summarizing the methods, experimental setup, results, and conclusions about OPCA's performance relative to baselines.

Current SubTask: Implement OPCA (Oscillatory Phase Credit Assignment): forward phase with eligibility trace storage, credit phase propagating backward using W (not W^T) with damping factor alpha, and weight update rule. (step 5)

self.beta2 = beta2\n        self.eps = eps\n        self.t = 0\n        self.m = {k: torch.zeros(s, device=device) for k, s in param_shapes.items()}\n        self.v = {k: torch.zeros(s, device=device) for k, s in param_shapes.items()}\n    \n    def step(self, grads, lr):\n        \"\"\"Apply Adam update.
